<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">      return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> lang</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    loadedLangs.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">set</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(lang, pending)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> pending</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> EXTENSION_LANGS</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> Record</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/** Guess a syntax language id from a filename like `app/page.tsx`. */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> languageFromFilename</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">filename</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">fallback</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "text"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> ext</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> filename?.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">split</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"."</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">).</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">pop</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">()?.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">toLowerCase</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (ext </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> EXTENSION_LANGS</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">[ext]) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> fallback</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Tokenize `code` with light and dark colours. Returns `null` until the</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * first pass resolves. While code streams in, the previous result is kept so</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * already-coloured lines never flash back to plain text.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> useCodeTokens</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">code</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">language</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> CodeTokenLines</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> key</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> `${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">language</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">\u0000</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">${</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">code</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">}`</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">result</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">setResult</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> React.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">useState</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;{ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">key</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">lines</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> CodeTokenLines</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">|</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> null</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> cached</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> tokenCache.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">get</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(key)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> cached </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { key, lines: cached } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> null</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  })</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  React.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">useEffect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> cached</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> tokenCache.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">get</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(key)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (cached) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">      setResult</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">({ key, lines: cached })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">      return</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> cancelled </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> false</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">    getHighlighter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      .</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">then</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">async</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">hl</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">        const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> lang</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> await</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> ensureLanguage</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(hl, language)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">        if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (cancelled) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">return</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">/**</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * Render one source line from (possibly stale) tokens. Tokens are used only</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * while they still describe a prefix of the line; anything past that renders</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> * as plain text until the next pass catches up.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E"> */</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">tokens </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> tokens.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> ===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> &#x3C;></span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">text</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;/></span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> LineSelector</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">start</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">end</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">number</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">]</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> toLineSet</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">lines</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> LineSelector</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">[]) {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    else</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> i </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Math.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">min</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(l[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">], l[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">]); i </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x3C;=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Math.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">max</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(l[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">], l[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">]); i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">++</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) set.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">add</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(i)</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">type</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> AgentCodeBlockProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> Omit</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">React</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">ComponentProps</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"div"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">>, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF">"children"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
